Working with Backdated Leave Requests

Source: https://tech-docs.prismhr.com/SP_help/Content/PD/HR/Action/2019/Backdated_Leave_Request_Processing.htm Path: PD/HR/Action/2019/Backdated_Leave_Request_Processing.htm

Working with Backdated Leave Requests

You can process approved, backdated leave requests for salaried employees by creating a manual check adjustment using the Backdated Leave Request Processing form.

[Image: screenshot]

To display on this form, an employee must be in a payroll batch with the Time Sheet Entry status. Also, the batch pay period dates must be later than the backdated leave request dates to be processed using Backdated Leave Request Processing functionality.

The manual check created from this process backs out the hours and associated dollars from the Offset Pay Code and assigns them to the Leave Pay Code using the appropriate codes on the Time Off Pay form. It also updates the employee’s Time Off register with the appropriate hours taken and includes other details from the Leave Request.

To process a backdated leave request:

  1. Check the Select All checkbox to process all the requests or check individual requests.

  2. Click Save.

  3. A dialog displays stating that clicking Yes will process the approved, backdated leave request(s), then a manual check adjustment for each selected request will be created to reduce the offset pay code hours by the absence code hours. Clicking No cancels the process.

  4. A confirmation dialog displays stating that the request(s) was successfully processed, and changes will be reflected in the next payroll. Click OK.

  5. Click View Report to view the Backdated Leave Request Report.

  6. After closing the report, successfully processed records no longer display in the form.

Note: Note: To enable Backdated Leave Request Processing for Worksite Managers and Trusted Advisors, set the ALLOWWSMWTABDLR custom feature code at the system level. (See Using Custom Feature Codes.)

Viewing Pending Actions

To display the number of employees in a regular payroll with pending backdated leave requests, click the yellow indicator in the Pending Actions column on the Time Sheet Entry, Process Payroll, and SuperBatch forms. (A form displays that provides more details.)

Note: Note that backdated leave requests only display on the Backdated Leave Request Processing form in the following conditions:

  • Only salaried employees display.

  • The backdated leave request must be approved.

  • The date of the backdated leave request must occur before the current timesheet pay period.

  • The employee must be part of the current timesheet.

  • The payroll for that timesheet must be in Time Entry status.

  • The payroll is a regular batch.

  • The backdated leave request pending actions do not display on the SuperBatch form when there are no pending approvals.

Changing an Employee’s Department

Source: https://tech-docs.prismhr.com/SP_help/Content/PD/HR/Action/2019/Department_Change_Form.htm Path: PD/HR/Action/2019/Department_Change_Form.htm

Changing an Employee’s Department

Use the Department Change form to change an employee’s department. For example:

[Image: screenshot]

To change an employee’s department:

  1. Enter or select the Employee by either typing the name or ID to display a pop-up list of matching employee records, or by clicking the field label to open the search window. The Current Employment information displays.

  2. Enter or select the New Department for the employee.

  3. Enter the Department Start Date when the new department goes into effect.

  4. Click Save.

Setting up Changes to an Employee’s Benefit Group

Source: https://tech-docs.prismhr.com/SP_help/Content/PD/HR/Action/2020/Benefit_Group_Maintenance.htm Path: PD/HR/Action/2020/Benefit_Group_Maintenance.htm

Setting up Changes to an Employee’s Benefit Group

You can set up a change to an employee’s benefit group using the Benefit Group Maintenance form.

To set up a proposed change to an employee’s benefit group, do the following:

  1. Go to the Benefit Group Maintenance form on the HR|Action menu.

  2. Enter the Employee name or click this field and select it from the Employee Search Results for Active Employees list.

  3. Click New Benefit Group and select a benefit group from the Benefit Group list. The benefit group number displays in the field, along with its description.

  4. The Benefit Group History panel also displays where you can view the effective dates and codes for changes to that employee’s benefit group.

  5. Enter the effective date in the Benefit Group Start Date field or select a date.

  6. Click Save to save the changes to the record.

  7. Click Cancel to clear the form.

Updating or Deleting the Future Effective Date of an Employee’s Benefit Group Change

You can update the effective date of a change to an employee’s benefit group or delete the change using the Benefit Group History panel.

To update or delete the effective date for a proposed change to an employee’s benefit group, do the following:

  1. In the Benefit Group History panel, click the future date you want to change or delete in the Date Effective column.

  2. To change the effective date, click the future date hyperlink. The New Effective Date dialog box displays.

a. Enter or select the new effective date in the New Effective Date field.
b. Click Save. The list updates with the new effective date.
— —
c. Click Cancel to clear the New Effective Date field.
— —
d. Click Close to close the dialog box without making any updates.
— —
  1. To remove the effective date, select the checkbox against the future date that you want to delete and click Delete. A confirmation message stating Benefit Group change will be deleted. Are you sure? displays.
a. Click Yes to delete the record.
b. Click No to close the confirmation message and remain on the form.
— —

Note:

For entering a past date in theBenefit Group Start Datefield:

  • If a Benefit Group History record currently exists with an Effective Date equal to the past date entered in the Benefit Group Start Date field, you will get a warning message stating A change record with an effective date equal to already exists. If you continue the existing record will be replaced. Do you wish to continue? On the warning message, when you click:

  • Yes - The warning message will close. All previously entered information will be displayed on the Benefit Group Change form. When you click Save on the Benefit Group Change form, the prior benefit group history record will be replaced with the current data.

  • No - The warning message will close. All previously entered information will be displayed on the Benefit Group Change form EXCEPT for the Benefit Group Start Date field which will remain blank. No benefit group history record will be written.

  • If a Benefit Group History record currently exists with an Effective Date greater than the past date entered in the Benefit Group Start Date field, you will get a warning message stating A future dated change record with an effective date equal to currently exists. Are you sure you want to continue? On the warning message, when you click:

  • Yes - The warning message will close. All previously entered information will be displayed on the Benefit Group Change form. When you click Save on the Benefit Group Change form, the prior benefit group history record will be replaced with the current data.

  • No - The warning message will close. All previously entered information will be displayed on the Benefit Group Change form EXCEPT for the Benefit Group Start Date field which will remain blank. No benefit group history record will be written.

  • If a Benefit Group History record currently exists with an Effective Date equal to the past date AND a Benefit Group History record exists with an Effective Date greater than the past date entered in the Benefit Group Start Date field, you will get a warning message stating A change record with an effective date equal to already exists. If you continue the existing record will be replaced. There is also a future dated change record with an effective date equal to . Do you wish to continue? On the warning message, when you click:

  • Yes - The warning message will close. All previously entered information will be displayed on the Benefit Group Change form. When you click Save on the Benefit Group Change form, the prior benefit group history record will be replaced with the current data.

  • No - The warning message will close. All previously entered information will be displayed on the Benefit Group Change form EXCEPT for the Benefit Group Start Date field which will remain blank. No benefit group history record will be written.

For entering a current date in theBenefit Group Start Datefield:

  • If a Benefit Group History record currently exists with an Effective Date equal to the current date entered in the Benefit Group Start Date field, you will get a warning message stating A change record with an effective date equal to already exists. If you continue the existing record will be replaced. Do you wish to continue? On the warning message, when you click:

  • Yes - The warning message will close. All previously entered information will be displayed on the Benefit Group Change form. When you click Save on the Benefit Group Change form, the prior benefit group history record will be replaced with the current data. When you exit the Benefit Group Change form, the new Benefit Group will display immediately in the Benefit Group field on the Employee Details form, under the Work tab, under the Assignments section.

  • No - The warning message will close. All previously entered information will be displayed on the Benefit Group Change form EXCEPT for the Benefit Group Start Date field which will remain blank. No benefit group history record will be written.

  • If a Benefit Group History record currently exists with an Effective Date greater than the current date entered in the Benefit Group Start Date field, you will get a warning message stating A future dated change record with an effective date equal to currently exists. Are you sure you want to continue? On the warning message, when you click:

  • Yes - The warning message will close. All previously entered information will be displayed on the Benefit Group Change form. When you click Save on the Benefit Group Change form, the prior benefit group history record will be replaced with the current data.

  • No - The warning message will close. All previously entered information will be displayed on the Benefit Group Change form EXCEPT for the Benefit Group Start Date field which will remain blank. No benefit group history record will be written.

  • For entering a future date in theBenefit Group Start Datefield:

Managing Pending Approvals

Source: https://tech-docs.prismhr.com/SP_help/Content/PD/HR/Action/2020/Managing_Pending_Approvals.htm Path: PD/HR/Action/2020/Managing_Pending_Approvals.htm

Managing Pending Approvals

Service providers, worksite managers, and worksite trusted providers can use the Manage Pending Approvals form to perform the following tasks:

  • Reassign pending approvals to other users.

  • Return a pending approval to the queue when the approval policy is based on a role, which allows any user with that role to approve it. (This is useful since you do not need to select a person to perform the pending approval.)

  • Reassign a pending approval to a specific user or return the approval to the Approvals Pending queue. (See the step on using the Assignment Options panel, which is described below.)

Important! Worksite managers can manage approvals for any users who have access to the client using the Manage Pending Approvals form. In addition, Worksite managers and worksite trusted advisors are required to limit user access to approvals for only those employees/payrolls where they are granted appropriate security access.

[Image: screenshot]

Note: This form replaces the Reassign Pending Approvals form. If you enter the old form name in the search box and press Enter, the Manage Pending Approvals form automatically displays.

You can reassign the following types of approvals:

  • Pending approvals of service providers, worksite managers, and worksite trusted advisors assignees

  • All approval types, including I-9 Section 2

  • Allows reassignment to client users whose access is identified in the Customer Users with Access panel on the Security tab of the Client Details form

  • Multi-step approvals: Retains approval for previous steps and allows reassignment of the next available step

To manage pending approvals:

  1. From HR|Action, select Manage Pending Approvals.

  2. Complete the following fields in the Manage Pending Approvals panel:

Field Description
Approval Type Select the Approval Type option for reassignment. (Note: The default for this field changed from Select to All.) > Note: Note the following: Users with FULL access to the Manage Pending Approvals form can do the following when ACH Maximum or Late Payroll Posting is selected: - View and reassign pending ACH Maximum or Late Payroll Posting Approvals for the selected client. - Filter pending approvals for ACH Maximum or Late Payroll Posting Approvals. Users with INQUIRY access to the Manage Pending Approvals form can do the following when ACH Maximum or Late Payroll Posting is selected: - Only view pending ACH Maximum or Late Payroll Posting Approvals for the selected client. - Cannot filter the form results. Note: Worksite managers and worksite trusted advisors are required to limit user access to approvals for only those employees/payrolls where they are granted appropriate security access.
  • View and reassign pending ACH Maximum or Late Payroll Posting Approvals for the selected client.
  • Filter pending approvals for ACH Maximum or Late Payroll Posting Approvals.
  • Only view pending ACH Maximum or Late Payroll Posting Approvals for the selected client.
  • Cannot filter the form results. | Policy Type | Select the policy type. After making a selection, the approval’s original policy type from the approval policy displays in the Approvals Pending table. For example, if an approval policy is set up as role-based and the user selects the approval, the system displays Role as the policy type. (Note: The default for this field is All.) > Note: Note the following when using this field: - If you select one or more approvals that have the Role policy type, then Return to Approval Queue (only for role-based policies) (see description below) becomes available. Selecting that option returns the approval to the approvals pending available to you section of the original role, and no longer displays in the Manage Pending Approvals form. - For multi-step approvals, the policy type reflects the original policy type of each step. For example, if step 1 is set to User and has been approved, then moves to step 2 as Role. After a user grabs step 2, the policy type on the form displays as Role. |
  • If you select one or more approvals that have the Role policy type, then Return to Approval Queue (only for role-based policies) (see description below) becomes available. Selecting that option returns the approval to the approvals pending available to you section of the original role, and no longer displays in the Manage Pending Approvals form.
  • For multi-step approvals, the policy type reflects the original policy type of each step. For example, if step 1 is set to User and has been approved, then moves to step 2 as Role. After a user grabs step 2, the policy type on the form displays as Role. | Assignee (User ID) | Enter or select the service provider, worksite manager, or worksite trusted advisor currently assigned as the pending approver. If you select this field, you can search for a user ID, employee ID, name, or email address from the Select User form that displays. In addition, select the User Type to filter the search further. Note: This field was originally named Assignee. |
  1. In the Approvals Pending table, select each pending approval for reassignment or click the box next to the Assignee field to select all pending approvals in this panel. The columns display the assignee’s information, such as the Due Date, which represents I-9 Section 2 pending approvals.

Note: The information in the Approvals Pending table displays in descending order by Date Created.

  1. In the Assignment Options panel, select one of the following fields:
Field Description
Return to Approval Queue (only for role-based policies) Select this field to return the approval to the queue. (Note: An error message displays if you select the User policy type after this field was already selected with the Role policy type approval. Also, the system displays an error if you select Update Assignments because you cannot move a User policy type approval back to the queue.) Note: The system returns a pending approval to the Approvals Pending (Available to You) Queue when the pending approval meets the following conditions: - Original Approval Policy is set up for either an HR Role or AM Role (cannot be User ID) - Pending approval was grabbed by a user and has an assignee Important! You cannot return a pending approval to the approval queue if the approval policy is assigned to a specific user ID.
  • Original Approval Policy is set up for either an HR Role or AM Role (cannot be User ID)
  • Pending approval was grabbed by a user and has an assignee | Reassign To (User ID) | Enter or select the user to reassign as the pending approver. If you select this field, you can search for a user ID, employee ID, name, or email address from the Select User form that displays after selecting this field, or enter this information. (Note: This is the user to reassign as the pending approver.) |
  1. Click Update Assignments. A confirmation dialog displays. Click Yes to reassign the pending approvals. Click Yes to confirm the updates.

Note: Click Clear to clear all selected entries/information in the form.

Processing Mass Employee Changes

Source: https://tech-docs.prismhr.com/SP_help/Content/PD/HR/Action/2020/Mass_Employee_HR_Actions.htm Path: PD/HR/Action/2020/Mass_Employee_HR_Actions.htm

Processing Mass Employee Changes

Use this feature to process leave of absences, employment reactivations, rehires, status type changes, and terminations for multiple employees at once, including reassigning pending approvals assigned to terminated employees. For example, if your client employs seasonal employees whom they terminate at the end of the season, you can select the Termination option to terminate those employees in a single process. For example:

[Image: screenshot]

Note: Note: This form replaces the Mass Employee Termination form.

Viewing an Employee’s Status History

Before processing multiple employee changes, you can view their status to determine how that employee will be impacted by the action you are about to process.

To view the status history for an employee, do the following:

  1. Go to the Mass Employee H/R Actions form on the HR|Action menu.

  2. Select Status Type History from the Actions menu. The Employee Status History form displays.

  3. Select or enter the Employee ID. All status history for that employee displays.

  4. Click Close.

Performing Mass Employee Actions

After viewing the status history for the employees you want to update, you can process a leave of absence, employment reactivation, rehires, status type change, or termination for multiple employees.

To perform a mass employee action, do the following:

  1. Go to the Mass Employee H/R Actions form on the HR|Action menu and select an option in the H/R Action field:
Option Description
Leave of Absence Processes multiple employee leave of absences at once
Reactivation Reactivates multiple employees at once
Rehire Processes multiple employee rehires at once
Status Type Change Processes multiple employee status type changes at once
Termination Processes multiple employee terminations at once

Note: You cannot edit the H/R Action or Employee Selection Method after making a selection in these fields. (Click Cancel to clear the form.)

  1. Select one of the following Employee Selection Method options:
Option Description
Generate List Generates a list of employees who meet the selected criteria.
Import List Displays the Upload Employees button, which you can select to import your list of employees who meet the selected criteria. > Note: Note the following when working with Import List: - The system only accepts uploaded files as a single-column list of employee IDs in either .txt or .csv format. - Employees listed in the import file who do not exist in the selected client are automatically excluded from the import. - The system validates the contents of the imported employee list and if any employee does not pass validation, they are not included in the on-screen report. In addition, if “Fail” displays in the Import Status column in the report, this indicates that the employee does not match the selected criteria. (You can access this report by selecting Download Report.) - If no employees pass validation a message displays, “There were no employees with selected.”

  • The system only accepts uploaded files as a single-column list of employee IDs in either .txt or .csv format.

  • Employees listed in the import file who do not exist in the selected client are automatically excluded from the import.

  • The system validates the contents of the imported employee list and if any employee does not pass validation, they are not included in the on-screen report. In addition, if “Fail” displays in the Import Status column in the report, this indicates that the employee does not match the selected criteria. (You can access this report by selecting Download Report.)

  • If no employees pass validation a message displays, “There were no employees with selected.” > Note: Note the following:

  • You must select an H/R Action and Employee Selection Method first before entering or selecting the other required fields.

  • After selecting an option, a confirmation message states that this functionality is only available to service providers and skips any approval policies already in place. Click OK to continue.

  • You must complete the required fields to generate a list of employees or display the Upload Employees button.

  1. Select the H/R Action Status field and choose a status for this action from the list that displays.

  2. For reactivations, rehires, and status type changes only, select the H/R Action Type field and choose an employee type code from the list that displays.

  3. Enter or select a date to perform the action in the H/R Action Date field.

  4. Select the H/R Action Reason field and choose a reason for this action from the list that displays.

  5. A list of employees or the Upload Employees button displays, depending on the Employee Selection Method option you selected.

Note: If you select Upload Employees, a File Upload dialog displays where you can select your employees list for import. Click Add Files and navigate to your file, then click Start Upload. After clicking Close the imported employees displays.

  1. Do the following depending on the action:
Action Description
Leave of Absence Enter or select a date in the Planned Return from Leave field when the employees will return from their leave of absence. (Note that a planned return is not required.)
Rehire Select the following fields, depending on the rehire action: - Stop Previous Voluntary Deductions: Stop any voluntary deductions that were previously set up for these employees. - Stop Previous Direct Deposit: Stop any direct deposits that were previously set up for these employees. - Auto-Enroll in Benefits (Medical, Retirement, PTO): Auto-enrolls employees into Medical, Retirement, or PTO plans that have auto-enroll rules configured at the client level. With rehires, this option only works if these employees do not have an existing Benefit/PTO record. - Do not launch Benefits Enrollment for rehire: Does not launch Benefits Enrollment for these employees.
  • Stop Previous Voluntary Deductions: Stop any voluntary deductions that were previously set up for these employees.
  • Stop Previous Direct Deposit: Stop any direct deposits that were previously set up for these employees.
  • Auto-Enroll in Benefits (Medical, Retirement, PTO): Auto-enrolls employees into Medical, Retirement, or PTO plans that have auto-enroll rules configured at the client level. With rehires, this option only works if these employees do not have an existing Benefit/PTO record.
  • Do not launch Benefits Enrollment for rehire: Does not launch Benefits Enrollment for these employees. | Status Type Change | Use the optional filter for Status and Type to narrow the Find Employees results. | | Termination | Do the following: - Enter or select Filter by Last Paid Date to filter all employees who have not been paid since this date. - Select Enroll employees with COBRA-electable benefits in COBRA if these employees need to enroll in this plan. - In the Okay to Rehire field, select Yes if these employees are eligible for rehire in the future. - Select If ACH, Turn off ACH? if you do not want these employees to receive further ACH transactions. The system sets the employees’ ACH status to Inactive. - Enter or select the Last Day Worked for these employees. |
  • Enter or select Filter by Last Paid Date to filter all employees who have not been paid since this date.
  • Select Enroll employees with COBRA-electable benefits in COBRA if these employees need to enroll in this plan.
  • In the Okay to Rehire field, select Yes if these employees are eligible for rehire in the future.
  • Select If ACH, Turn off ACH? if you do not want these employees to receive further ACH transactions. The system sets the employees’ ACH status to Inactive.
  • Enter or select the Last Day Worked for these employees.

  1. To perform this action for all employees, click Select All. Otherwise, select each employee as a recipient of this action.

  2. Click Process to perform the action. A confirmation box displays. Click Yes to continue.

  3. A message displays in the form confirming the action is complete.

Reassigning Pending Approvals for Terminated Employees

During the termination process, the system detects if any employees being terminated have pending approvals. If the terminated employees do have pending approvals a prompt displays after the termination is complete, “There are pending approvals assigned to terminated employees. Do you want to reassign the pending approvals to other client users?”

To reassign pending approvals for terminated employees, do the following:

  1. Click Yes.

  2. The Manage Pending Approvals form displays, where you can reassign pending approvals to other users.

Note: For more information, see Managing Pending Approvals.

Inactivating Mass Employee Direct Deposit Records

Source: https://tech-docs.prismhr.com/SP_help/Content/PD/HR/Action/2020/Mass_Inactivate_Employee_ACH_Record.htm Path: PD/HR/Action/2020/Mass_Inactivate_Employee_ACH_Record.htm

Inactivating Mass Employee Direct Deposit Records

Use this feature to locate all employees who have not been paid as of a certain date and inactivate their direct deposit records at once.

[Image: screenshot]

Performing Mass Employee Direct Deposit Inactivation

You can locate and inactivate direct deposit records for multiple employees using the Mass Inactivate Employee ACH Record form.

To use this feature, do the following:

  1. Go to the Mass Inactivate Employee ACH Record form on the HR|Action menu.

  2. Select or enter a date in the Employee Not Paid As Of field. A list of employees associated with that date displays.

  3. If necessary, select an appropriate option in the Employment Status field to filter employee records for the client (the default is ALL).

  4. To inactivate the direct deposit records for all employees in the list, click Select All. Otherwise, select each employee as a recipient of this action.

  5. Click Inactivate ACH. A confirmation dialog displays.

  6. Click Yes to continue. A message displays confirming that the records for the selected employees were updated.

Deleting Mass Employee Time Off Registers

Source: https://tech-docs.prismhr.com/SP_help/Content/PD/HR/Action/2021/Mass_Delete_Employee_PTO_Registers.htm Path: PD/HR/Action/2021/Mass_Delete_Employee_PTO_Registers.htm

Deleting Mass Employee Time Off Registers

Use this feature to delete Time Off registers for multiple employees at once. For example, you can use this process if your client restructures their time off (including the register) and wants to remove all terminated employees’ bank records.

[Image: screenshot]

Note: Note the following when using the Mass Delete Employee Time Off Registers form:

  • This form is only available for service providers.

  • After you delete an employee Time Off register, the system permanently deletes a posted record of all accrued, used, and carryover time off for the employees in the Time Off accrual schedule, so those affected employees will no longer be enrolled for this type of time off.

To perform a mass deletion of employee Time Off registers, do the following:

  1. Go to the Mass Delete Employee Time Off Registers form on the HR|Action menu and complete the following fields:

Field — Description

Accrual Schedule — Enter or select the Time Off accrual schedule for your active client. If you select this field, the Select Accrual Schedule form displays. You can either select from the list that displays or search for a specific Time Off accrual schedule by entering it in the field. After you select one of the accrual schedules, the form closes and it displays in the Accrual Schedule field back in the Mass Delete Employee Time Off Registers form. Note: The list of Time Off accrual schedules that display are the same as those available to the client in the Time Off Type Code field on the Time Off Accrual Schedules form.

Time Off Plan — Enter or select the Time Off plan for your active client. If you select this field, the Select Time Off Plans form displays. You can either select from the list that displays or search for a specific Time Off plan by entering it in the field. After you select one of the plans, the form closes and it displays in the Time Off Plan field back in the Mass Delete Employee Time Off Registers form. Note: The list of Time Off plans that display are the same as those available to the client in the Plan Code field on the Time Off Plans form.

  1. Select Find Employees. In the employee list that displays, you can Select All employees or make individual selections from the list.

Note: If you select a different Time Off accrual schedule or Time Off plan after selecting Find Employees, the following message displays, “Selecting a new Time Off Accrual Schedule or Time Off Plan will clear the current list of employees. Do you wish to continue?” Click Yes to clear the list of employees or No to cancel.

  1. Click Delete Registers. A confirmation message displays, “You are about to delete <number> employee Time Off registers and all associated records and enrollments. Do you want to continue?” Select one of the following:

  • Yes: Deletes all Time Off registers for the selected employees. (Note: After you make this selection, the Employee Time Off Register form no longer contains any evidence of this record.)

  • No: Cancels the deletion and returns to the form where the Time Off accrual schedule and Time Off plan selections still display.

Note: Note the following before clicking Delete Registers:

  • If you click Delete Registers without selecting the Time Off accrual schedule or Time Off plan, the following message displays, “Registers can only be deleted after selecting employees. Select a Time Off Accrual Schedule and Time Off Plan to list employees.”

  • If you click Delete Registers without selecting an employee, the following message displays, “Select at least one employee register to delete.”

Clearing New Hire Reporting Dates

Source: https://tech-docs.prismhr.com/SP_help/Content/PD/HR/Action/Clear%20New%20Hire%20Reporting%20Dates.htm Path: PD/HR/Action/Clear New Hire Reporting Dates.htm

Clearing New Hire Reporting Dates

If you enter a new hire reporting date in an employee record, the employee does not display on the New Hire Reporting download. The Clear New Hire Reporting Dates process clears the new hire reporting dates, so the employees display on the subsequent New Hire Reporting download. For example, if you are supporting international employees, they initially use a temporary SSN during the application period. When the permanent SSN is issued, you might need to clear the New Hire Reporting Date so those employees are included in the next reporting cycle.

Use the Clear New Hire Reporting Dates form to clear the New Hire Reporting Date for a specified date range for one or all clients. You can also use this form to clear the New Hire Reporting Date for a specified date range for one or more employees for a selected client.

Note: Note: Client Access Group Security is enabled on this form.

To access the form, in the Work Centers panel, under the heading Compliance, click New Hire and Termination Reporting. Then under the heading Reporting Date Overrides, click Clear New Hire Reporting Dates.

To clear new hire reporting dates:

  1. Complete the following

Field Name — Description

Starting New Hire Report Date — Enter the starting date of the reporting date range from when you want to clear New Hire Reporting Date.

Ending New Hire Report Date — Enter the ending date of the reporting date range till when you want to clear New Hire Reporting Date.

Client ID — This field is optional. Leave it blank to select all clients. If you want to clear the New Hire Reporting Date for employees for a particular client, enter it here or click the Client ID hyperlink to select it from the Select Client dialog. > Note: Note: Once you enter a Client ID here, the following field becomes visible.

Employee — This field is optional. Leave it blank to select all employees for the selected client. Here you can enter one or more employees for whom you want to clear the New Hire Reporting Date for the selected date range, or click the Employee hyperlink and select one or more employees from the Select Employee dialog.

  1. Click Process. A progress window opens that displays the number of records read and the number of records cleared, and notifies you when the process is complete. The Close and Report buttons remain disabled while the report is being generated.

  2. Click Close to close the window.

  3. Click Report to display the Job Control Status Report window.

  4. In the Job Control Status Report window, click the XLS link to download the report.

Performing Employer Reassignment

Source: https://tech-docs.prismhr.com/SP_help/Content/PD/HR/Action/Employer_Reassignment.htm Path: PD/HR/Action/Employer_Reassignment.htm

Performing Employer Reassignment

You can assign an existing employee to another employer using the Employer Reassignment form. It must be an employer that is valid for the client.

[Image: Employer reassignment]

Note: Note: You cannot change the Client ID to which the employee is assigned. You can reassign only the Employer ID.

To assign an existing employee to another employer, do the following:

  1. Enter the Employee by either entering the name, Social Security Number, or ID to display a pop-up list of matching employee records. You can also click the field label or press Ctrl+Enter with your cursor in this field to open the search window.

  2. Click the Employer ID. The Employer Reassignment Maintenance window opens.

[Image: Assigning an employee to a new employer]

  1. Enter the New Employer ID.

  2. Click Save. The new employer displays.

Tracking Employee Leaves of Absence

Source: https://tech-docs.prismhr.com/SP_help/Content/PD/HR/Action/Leave_of_Absence.htm Path: PD/HR/Action/Leave_of_Absence.htm

Tracking Employee Leaves of Absence

Use the Leave of Absence form to track an employee’s leave of absence while maintaining the employee’s status of employment at your company. This is used for absences that are not covered by PTO registers.

Note: Note: To set multiple employees on leave of absence at once, see Processing Mass Employee Changes.

[Image: screenshot]

Note: The PTO Accruals panel only displays when the following conditions are met:

  • The entered or selected employee is enrolled in one or more PTO registers.

  • You enter a Leave Effective Date that is either the current day or a date before that day.

To track an employee’s leave of absence, do the following:

  1. Enter the Employee by either entering the name, Social Security Number, or ID to display a pop-up list of matching employee records. You can also click the field label or press Ctrl+Enter with your cursor in this field to open the search window. The employee’s current status and type display.

  2. Select the Leave Employment Status for the employee.

Note: This field displays the full description of the status code, separated by a dash (-) in front of the status code.

  1. Enter the Leave Effective Date.

  2. Select the Reason Code for the leave of absence.

  3. Enter the Planned Return from Leave date when the employee plans to return to work.

  4. To stop PTO accruals for the employee, select the Stop PTO Accruals field for the PTO Register Type. (The PTO Register Type and PTO Benefit Plan columns display the PTO Register and Plan where the employee is enrolled.)

Note: After you save this selection and there is a leave of absence approval policy in place, when the leave of absence is approved this setting registers in the Stop Time Off Accruals field on the Employee Time Off Register form.

  1. Click Save.

Note: Note: Use Reactivation to return an employee to regular employment status.

Actions Menu

You can access information about the Employee’s Status history by clicking Status Type History in the Actions menu.

  • Status Type History: see Viewing Employment Status Updates for Employees

Related Topics

Reactivating an Employee

Performing Status Type Changes

Making a Location Change for Multiple Employees

Source: https://tech-docs.prismhr.com/SP_help/Content/PD/HR/Action/Mass_Location_Change.htm Path: PD/HR/Action/Mass_Location_Change.htm

Making a Location Change for Multiple Employees

You can set a new location for multiple employees at once using the Mass Location Change form.

The filtering options in this form allow you to find employees based on specific criteria related to the employee’s status, type, location, and so on.

To make a mass location change, do the following:

  1. From HR|Action, select Mass Location Change and complete these panels as required:
Panel Description
Filters Set the filters for the employee search, then select Find Employees to list all employees who meet the criteria. (All filters include obsolete codes during a search.)
Select Employees In the list that displays, either choose all employees by selecting the box under the Select Employees header or select each employee individually.
Location Change Actions Select the location change in the Location Change Actions panel as follows: - New Location: Enter or select the New Location per pay period. (Note: If you select this field, the Search filter does not display obsolete codes.) - Effective Date: Enter the Effective Date per pay period (defaults to the current date). (Note: You cannot enter a past date.)
  • New Location: Enter or select the New Location per pay period. (Note: If you select this field, the Search filter does not display obsolete codes.)
  • Effective Date: Enter the Effective Date per pay period (defaults to the current date). (Note: You cannot enter a past date.)
  1. Select Preview Changes. The Preview of Location Changes Report displays the employees with the requested location change. Close the report.

Note: You can preview the changes until they are committed to the employee record. In addition, you can export the report as a PDF or XLS file.

  1. Click Process Changes, then click Close after the system completes the changes. The Download Confirmation Report link displays.

Note: Note the following:

  • Before you can access the report from this link, the system displays “Select the Download confirmation report link below to generate a file of the employee(s) with location changes.” Click OK to continue.

  • If you process changes using the current date, the system immediately writes the new location information to the employee’s record, which does not display on the Location History form.

  • If you process changes using a future date, the new location displays on the Location History form with the future effective date, and the system updates the employee’s record as of that date.

  • The system does not automatically update the current timesheet with the location changes made on the Mass Location Change form. However, these location changes do display on the current timesheet after you delete and recreate the payroll.

  • If you deselect a checkbox in the employee list or select all employees when individual employees were previously selected, or you make any changes to the fields in the Location Change Actions panel, Process Changes becomes unavailable until you select Preview Changes again.

  • If at least one error occurs during processing in addition to the system successfully processing rate changes the message instead displays as follows, “Select the Download confirmation and Error reports from the location change process.” Click OK to continue.

  • If only errors occur during processing the message instead displays as follows, “Select the Error report link below to generate a file of the employee(s) that could not be updated.” Click OK to continue.

  1. Click the Download Confirmation Report link to display the link to the Location Update Confirmation Report in a separate window. Open the link for this CSV-formatted report to display the employees with the requested location change.

Note: If there is at least one employee location change error, the Download Error Report link displays. Open this link to display the Location Error Report, which provides the employee information (ID and name) and a description of the error. For example, if you try to change an employee’s location to their current location the following message displays, “The employee already has that location: XXX.” (Any employees listed in this report do not display in the Location Update Confirmation Report and no location change is made to their record.)

  1. Close the report to return to the Mass Location Change form. (Note: After the report closes, you can re-open it until you navigate away from the form or click Cancel.)

Note: Note the following:

  • If your user role access for the Mass Location Change form is set to Inquiry, the fields on this form display as read-only.

  • After finding employees, if you reset the filters to perform another search a warning message displays, “Selecting or updating the filters will clear the current list of employees. Do you want to continue?” Selecting Yes keeps the updated filter selections in place.

  • If you disable a field on this form, or select all fields, or make any changes in the form after selecting Preview Changes, the Process Rate Changes button is unusable until you select Preview Changes again.

  • The system displays up to 500 employees per page.

========================================================================

Making a Pay Rate Change for Multiple Employees

Source: https://tech-docs.prismhr.com/SP_help/Content/PD/HR/Action/Mass_Pay_Rate_Change.htm Path: PD/HR/Action/Mass_Pay_Rate_Change.htm

Making a Pay Rate Change for Multiple Employees

You can set a new pay rate for multiple employees at once using the Mass Pay Rate Change form.

The filtering options in this form allow you to find employees based on specific criteria related to the employee’s status, type, pay method, and so on.

Note: The Variable Salary option in the Pay Method field only displays if the VARSALMETHOD custom feature code is enabled on the System Parameters form.

To make a mass pay rate change, do the following:

  1. From HR|Action, select Mass Pay Rate Change and complete these panels as required:
Panel Description
Filters Set the filters for the employee search, then select Find Employees to list all employees who meet the criteria. (All filters include obsolete codes during a search.)
Select Employees In the list that displays, either choose all employees by selecting the box under the Select Employees header or select each employee individually.
Pay Rate Actions Select the pay rate change by the total amount or percentage increase in the Pay Rate Actions panel as follows: - Rate: Enter the New Pay Rate per pay period, Effective Date, and Reason Code. - Percentage: Enter the Percent Change per pay period, Effective Date, and Reason Code. Note: Service providers can optionally set the New Standard Hours per pay period for either of these rate change options.
  • Rate: Enter the New Pay Rate per pay period, Effective Date, and Reason Code.
  • Percentage: Enter the Percent Change per pay period, Effective Date, and Reason Code.
  1. Select Preview Changes. The Preview of Pay Rate Changes Report displays the employees with the requested pay rate change. Close the report.

Note: You can preview the changes until they are committed to the employee record. In addition, you can export the report as a PDF or XLS file.

  1. Click Process Rate Changes, then click Close after the system completes the changes. The Download Confirmation Report link displays.

Note: Note the following:

  • Before you can access the report from this link, the system displays “Select the Download confirmation report link below to generate a file of the employee(s) with pay rate changes.” Click OK to continue.

  • If at least one error occurs during processing in addition to the system successfully processing rate changes the message instead displays as follows, “Select the Download confirmation and Error report link below to generate a file of the employee(s) with pay rate changes.” Click OK to continue.

  • If only errors occur during processing the message instead displays as follows, “Select the Error report link below to generate a file of the employee(s) that could not be updated.” Click OK to continue.

  1. Click the Download Confirmation Report link to display the link to the Pay Rate Update Confirmation Report in a separate window. Open the link for this CSV-formatted report to display the employees with the requested pay rate change.

Note: If there is at least one employee pay rate change error, the Download Error Report link displays. Open this link to display the Pay Rate Error Report, which provides the employee information (ID and name) and a description of the error. (Any employees listed in this report do not display in the Pay Rate Update Confirmation Report and no pay rate change is made to their record.)

  1. Close the report to return to the Mass Pay Rate Change form. (Note: After the report closes, you can re-open it until you navigate away from the form or click Cancel.)

Note: Note the following:

  • If your user role access for the Mass Pay Rate Change form is set to Inquiry, the fields on this form display as read-only.

  • After finding employees, if you reset the filters to perform another search a warning message displays, “Selecting or updating the filters will clear the current list of employees. Do you want to continue?” Selecting Yes keeps the updated filter selections in place.

  • If you disable a field on this form, or select all fields, or make any changes in the form after selecting Preview Changes, the Process Rate Changes button is unusable until you select Preview Changes again.

  • The system displays up to 500 employees per page.

Setting Up New Hire Information

Source: https://tech-docs.prismhr.com/SP_help/Content/PD/HR/Action/New_Hire.htm Path: PD/HR/Action/New_Hire.htm

Setting Up New Hire Information

You can enter a newly hired employee’s information into PrismHR using the New Hire form.

Note: Note: Depending on the system parameters and the company’s settings, different fields might be required.

You can access job candidate information by selecting Candidates from the Actions menu

[Image: screenshot]

. Use the search dialog to select the job candidate you want; the candidate’s information populates the form.

  • Candidates: See Maintaining Job Candidates Information

  • New Hire Notes: See Entering New Hire Notes

Once you save the New Hire form, you can select Employee Details from the Actions menu

[Image: screenshot]

.

To set up new hire information, do the following:

To set up Complete this panel
Type of employee being hired New Hire
Personal information for the new employee Personal Information
Address and emergency contact information for the new employee Resident Address and Emergency Contact
Various details about when and where the new employee works Employment Details
Compensation details about the new employee Pay Details
Identity and employment eligibility information Form I-9 Details
Other information for the employee’s Form I-9 information Form I-9 Miscellaneous
Account information required for payroll ACH transactions Direct Deposit Information
Federal tax filing information and allowances for the employee Federal Form W4 Details
Resident and work state Form W-4 details, if required Resident State Form W4 Details and Work State Form W4 Details

The following sections describe how to set up new hire information in the panels that reside in the New Hire form.

Identifying Employee Type

You can identify the type of employee being hired using the New Hire panel.

To identify the employee type, complete the following fields:

Field — Description

Work Location — Enter or select the primary Work Location ID where the employee works. When there are mandatory new hire questions, a new form opens. Complete the form and click Submit Answers, then continue completing the New Hire form.

Employer ID — Select the Employer ID.

Social Security Number — Enter the new employee’s Social Security number. The Social Security Number field does not accept any values that begin with the numeral 9, but you can enter the ITIN for a 1099 Contractor which begins with the numeral 9. (All valid Individual Taxpayer Identification Numbers (ITINs) are a nine-digit number in the same format as the SSN (9XX–XX-XXXX), begins with a 9 and the 4th and 5th digits range from 50-65, 70-88, 90-92 and 94-99.) If you enter the ITIN of a 1099 contractor, you will receive a message stating The number you have entered is not a valid Social Security number. The new hire cannot be onboarded as an employee with this number. You should proceed only if you wish to enter this new hire as a contractor, and the new hire meets IRS requirements for using an Individual Taxpayer Identification Number in place of a social security number. Click OK to continue. The Hire Type Field will automatically change to Contractor (1099) on the New Hire form. > Note: Note: Custom Feature Code ALLOW999SSNS should not be enabled for the client. > Note: Note: On the Client Details form, on the Other tab, under Other Options the field named Contractors (1099) Allowed should be selected.

Hire Type — Select the employee Hire Type. The Use Onboarding field displays only if the company is set up to prompt for electronic onboarding.

Use Onboarding — From the Use Onboarding drop-down, indicate whether you are entering all of the employee’s information or if the employee will enter it in Employee Portal (EP). This affects which fields are required. Employee IDs are generated automatically at the creation of the employee record and they cannot be edited. Each ID consists of a letter followed by five digits.

Defining Personal Information

You can define the personal information for the new employee using the Personal Information panel.

Note: Note: The First Name, Last Name, Middle Name, and Preferred Name fields do not allow special characters.

To define the personal information for the new employee, complete the following fields:

Field — Description

First Name — Enter the new employee’s First Name.

Last Name — Enter the employee’s Last Name.

Middle Name — Enter the employee’s Middle Name.

Preferred Name — Enter the employee’s Preferred Name, which the system uses in place of their legal first name.

Nickname — Enter the employee’s Nickname.

Birth Date — Enter the employee’s Birth Date.

Gender — Select the employee’s Gender.

Ethnic Code — Select the employee’s Ethnic Code.

Marital Status — Select the employee’s Marital Status.

Tobacco User — Select whether the employee uses tobacco products. This information is used to calculate life insurance premiums.

For each of the following fields, select Yes, No, Not Selected, or Declined.

Field Description
Disabled Whether the employee is disabled, as defined by the Americans with Disabilities Act of 1990.
Veteran The employee is a United States veteran. - Vietnam Veteran: The employee is a United States veteran of the Vietnam war. - Disabled Veteran: The employee is a United States veteran who has a disability as defined by the Department of Labor VETS‑4212 Report. - Recently Separated Veteran:The employee is a United States veteran who has recently left the armed services. - Service Medal Veteran: The employee is a United States veteran who was presented with a service medal. - Other Protected Veteran: The employee is a United States veteran who is protected by the Department of Labor VETS‑4212 Report.
  • Vietnam Veteran: The employee is a United States veteran of the Vietnam war.
  • Disabled Veteran: The employee is a United States veteran who has a disability as defined by the Department of Labor VETS‑4212 Report.
  • Recently Separated Veteran:The employee is a United States veteran who has recently left the armed services.
  • Service Medal Veteran: The employee is a United States veteran who was presented with a service medal.
  • Other Protected Veteran: The employee is a United States veteran who is protected by the Department of Labor VETS‑4212 Report. ## Maintaining Resident Address and Emergency Contact

You can maintain the address and emergency contact information for the new employee using the Resident Address and Emergency Contact panels.

To maintain the address and emergency contact information for the new employee, complete the following fields:

Field Description
Address Line One/ Address Line Two Enter the employee’s street Address Line One and, if appropriate, Address Line Two.
ZIP Code Enter or select the ZIP Code for the employee’s residence. You will be presented with a pop-up of Geocode options when you enter a zip code that spans multiple cities, counties, or localities. Select the correct Geocode to update the address. After you enter or select the ZIP Code, the system checks the street address to make sure that it is valid. If there are typos, missing information, or other unrecognized values, the system displays a dialog suggesting a correction to the address. Click Yes to accept the corrected address, otherwise click No to save as-is or make other edits. Note: If you enter something that the system cannot match to any existing address, it displays the first address in the master list for the ZIP code (such as 1 Apple Drive).
GeoCode Select the Vertex GeoCode. The City, State, and County display.
Home Phone Enter the employee’s Home Phone number.
Mobile Phone Enter the employee’s Mobile Phone number.
Personal Email Address Enter the employee’s Personal Email Address. > Note: Note the following for the Personal Email Address and Work Email Address fields: - You cannot enter an invalid character in the email address fields. For example, if you enter a left bracket ([) in the user name segment in one of the email address fields and then move away from that field an error message displays, “Email address cannot contain illegal character: [.” (Note: The system does not remove the invalid character, so you need to make the correction.) - If you try to save an email address that contains an invalid character, the system disables the save function and an error message displays. - If an existing email address contains an invalid character and you do not remove it, when you click Save the system displays a warning message, but still allows you to save the information. For example, if there’s an invalid character in the Personal Email Address field the following warning displays, “Warning! Personal email address cannot contain illegal character: ‘X’. It should be updated to a valid email address.” Click OK to save the other information in the form (the invalid email address remains in the field).

You can establish compensation details about the new employee using the Pay Details panel.

To establish compensation details about the new employee, complete the following fields:

Field Description
Pay Group Enter or select the Pay Group to which the new employee belongs.
Pay Method Select the Pay Method by which the employee will be paid. The Pay Rate and Pay Rate Basis define the new employee’s pay. For example, $250 Weekly or $3500 Monthly. Note: The Variable Salary option only displays if the VARSALMETHOD custom feature code is enabled on the System Parameters form.
Pay Rate Enter the Pay Rate that corresponds with the selected Pay Rate Basis. Note: For an FLSA exempt new hire, if the annual salary (entered or calculated) in the Pay Rate field under the Pay Detailssection, is lower than the Minimum Exempt Salary Amount on the State Rules form for that state for the latest entered year, and you try to save the form, then you will receive a warning stating The employee has been assigned a pay rate that is below the [State Abbreviation] Minimum Exempt Salary Rate of [Amount]. This is a warning only. Continue? - Click Yes to save the change. - Click No to return to the Pay Rate field.
  • Click Yes to save the change.
  • Click No to return to the Pay Rate field. | Pay Rate Basis | Select the Pay Rate Basis that determines the employee’s pay period. | | Standard Hours | Enter the Standard Hours worked by the employee during the pay period. The system uses this to calculate the employee’s annualized pay. | | Auto Time Sheet | Select Auto Time Sheet if this is a salaried employee and PrismHR should automatically create and accept time sheets for the employee. | | Default Time Sheet Hours | Enter the Default Time Sheet Hours to use in conjunction with the Auto Time Sheet option. The employee’s time sheet defaults to that number of hours. | | First Pay Period Hours | If available, you can enter the First Pay Period Hours. This is typically used for employees with auto time sheet hours when their hire date falls in the middle of a payroll period. This feature must be enabled in the System Parameters and for the selected client. Once you enter the client and employer start dates, pay group, and standard hours, select Auto Time Sheet. A calendar displays the employee’s start date as well as the pay period start and end dates to help you determine the number of hours to enter for the first paycheck. | | Benefits Calculation Salary | Enter the Benefits Calculation Salary to use as an override for any benefit calculations that use employee’s salary in the calculation. Note: The value you enter in this field populates the Benefit Salary field in the Pay tab of the Employee Details form. | > Note: Note: The Form I-9, Direct Deposit, and Form W-4 panels display only if the system is set to have you enter all employee information. If the employee will enter the information during the onboarding process, proceed directly to saving the record; see Saving the New Hire Information.

You can enter other information for the employee’s Form I-9 using the Form I-9 Miscellaneous panel.

To enter other information for the employee’s Form I-9, complete the following fields:

Field — Description

I9 First Name — Enter the new employee’s first name as entered on the Form I-9.

I9 Middle Name — Enter the new employee’s middle name as entered on the Form I-9.

I9 Last Name — Enter the new employee’s last name as entered on the Form I-9.

I9 Other Name Used — Enter the new employee’s other name as entered on the Form I-9.

I9 Start Date — Enter or select the I9 Start Date as entered on the employee’s Form I-9.

I9 Renew Date — Enter or select the I9 Renew Date by which the employee must be re-certified, as entered on the employee’s Form I-9.

I9 SSN — Enter the I9 SSN as entered on the employee’s Form I-9.

I9 Date of Birth — Enter or select the I9 Date of Birth as entered on the employee’s Form I-9.

I9 Citizenship Status — Select the I9 Citizenship Status as entered on the employee’s Form I-9.

Alien Registration Number — Enter the Alien Registration Number on the employee’s “green card.”

Form I-94 Admission Number — Enter the Form I-94 Admission Number the employee was assigned upon entering the USA.

Foreign Passport Number — Enter the employee’s Foreign Passport Number.

Country of Issuance — Enter or select the foreign passport’s Country of Issuance.

Alien Reg Expiration Date — Enter or select the Alien Reg Expiration Date that was issued by the INS.

Non-Resident Alien — Select Non-Resident Alien, if appropriate.

Handbook Mailed On — Enter or select the Handbook Mailed On date.

Background Test Done — Select Background Test Done to indicate there was a background check performed on the employee.

Background Test Date — Enter or select the Background Test Date.

Ohio Form C112 Signed — Select Ohio Form C112 Signed to indicate that the employee signed the form.

Establishing Account Information

You can establish account information required for payroll ACH transactions using the Direct Deposit Information panel.

To establish account information required for payroll ACH transactions, complete the following fields:

Field — Description

Deposit Status — Select the Deposit Status, indicating whether PrismHR should generate an entry for the new employee’s pay voucher.

Pay Types for Deposit — Select the Pay Types for Deposit for which the system makes direct deposits.

Account Type — Select the bank Account Type.

Transit Number — Enter or select the ABA Transit Number of the receiving bank or other financial institution. The Bank Name displays.

Account Number — Enter the employee’s Account Number.

Pay Type Override — Select the Pay Type Override to which this line is limited. This overrides the Pay Types for Deposit value. If you leave the Pay Type Override field blank, this deposit is not limited to any voucher types.

Method — Select the Method that PrismHR will use to calculate the amount to be deposited into the specified bank account.

Amount — Enter the amount to deposit to the account, based on the Method.

Limit Amount — If the Method is set to Fixed, Percentage, or Fixed Skip, enter the Limit Amount that represents a maximum dollar amount of the percentage.

Deposit Status — Select the Deposit Status of this direct deposit account.

Establishing Federal Tax Filing Information

You can establish Federal tax filing information and allowances for the employee using the Federal Form W4 Details panel.

To establish Federal tax filing information and allowances for the employee, complete the following fields:

Field — Description

Filing Status — Select the new employee’s Federal tax Filing Status.

Allowances — Enter the number of Federal withholding Allowances (exemptions) claimed by the employee.

Additional Amount — Enter an Additional Amount, if any, to be withheld from each paycheck.

Exempt — Select Exempt if the new employee claims or should be exempt from federal taxes. Selecting this option will block federal withholding deductions from the employee’s paycheck.

Defining 2020 Form W-4 Details

If you are filling out a Form W-4 for 2020 or later, enter the appropriate information in the new 2020 Form W-4 fields:

Field — Description

Multiple Jobs — Select Multiple Jobs if the new hire meets the conditions needed to check the “Multiple Jobs” box on Form W-4. See the IRS Form W-4 instructions for more information.

Claim Dependents Credit — If appropriate, enter an amount for Claim Dependents Credit ($). See the Form W-4 instructions for more information.

Other Income — Enter any Other Income according to the instructions on Form W-4.

Deductions — Enter an amount for Deductions ($). See the IRS Form W-4 instructions for more information.

Defining Work and Resident State Form W-4 Details

Based on the new employee’s work location and resident address GeoCode, these panels display Form W-4 fields for that state, if any. Complete the information as necessary.

Saving the New Hire Information

After completing all the information on the New Hire form, click Save.

The results of saving the record differ, depending on the settings. For example, the employee might be immediately hired and become an active employee, or the new hire might be submitted for approval first. The system displays the ID generated for the new employee record.

Calculating Net Pay

Source: https://tech-docs.prismhr.com/SP_help/Content/PD/HR/Action/Pay_Check_Calc_Net_Pay.htm Path: PD/HR/Action/Pay_Check_Calc_Net_Pay.htm

Calculating Net Pay

Using the Pay Check Calculator, you can calculate the net pay for an employee. This does not initialize a voucher; you would use it to calculate the employee’s net pay amount.

To calculate net pay:

  1. Enter the Employee by either entering the name, Social Security Number, or ID to display a pop-up list of matching employee records. You can also click the field label or press Ctrl+Enter with your cursor in this field to open the search window.

The Pay Check Calculator panel displays the employee’s ID, name, and standard pay period (pay group).

  1. Select the Deduction Period that applies to the check, if any.

Recurring deductions for that period display in the Deductions panel after you enter hours or amounts in the Gross Pay panel.

[Image: screenshot]

  1. The Gross Pay panel initially lists the default pay codes for the client. You can enter values in those fields, and add additional rows as needed.

  • Select the Pay Code.

  • Enter the Charge Date.

  • Select the Position the employee worked.

  • As appropriate for the client, select the Shift or Project. (These columns are hidden if they are not specified in the Client Details > Time Sheet tab.)

Note: Note: The Shift and Project drop-downs display up to 1000 codes. If the code you need is not in the drop-down, click the link at the top of the column to search for the appropriate code.

  • Enter the Hours/Units to be paid.

  • The Pay Rate defaults from the employee’s information, but you can edit it if appropriate.

The system calculates and displays the Extended Pay.

[Image: example setup in the Gross Pay panel]

Note: Note: The system pulls the deductions from the employee’s Recurring Deductions record. If a new deduction is not yet included in that form, it is not listed here.

  1. The Deductions panel lists the employee’s deductions associated with the selected Deduction Period, if any, after you enter gross pay. You can make any edits and additions as needed, otherwise continue to the next step.

Each existing Deduction displays, as well as the Deduction Amount Calculated.

If you need to enter or edit deductions:

  • Select the Deduction, if needed.

  • Enter an amount in Deduction Amount Entered to apply an amount for a new deduction or change the amount for an existing deduction.

If you override the deduction amount, the Deduction Amount Calculated value changes to reflect the override amount you entered. If you edit 401(k) or Section 125 deductions, the tax amounts adjust to reflect the new sheltered earnings.

[Image: Deductions entered in the deduction grid]

  1. The Taxes panel displays the taxes to be deducted from the employee’s paycheck after you enter gross pay.

[Image: Taxes panel with OASDI and income taxes listed]

  1. The Pay Summary panel displays the totals from the other panels and the calculated net pay.

[Image: Pay summary calculation example]

  1. When you are finished, click Clear.

Pay Check Calculator

Source: https://tech-docs.prismhr.com/SP_help/Content/PD/HR/Action/Pay_Check_Calculator.htm Path: PD/HR/Action/Pay_Check_Calculator.htm

Calculating an Employee’s Pay

You can calculate the pay for an employee using the Pay Check Calculator form.

Note: The Pay Check Calculator form uses either the Deduction Period or manually-entered deductions in the Deductions panel when calculating taxable amounts, but it does not do both. For example:

  • If the Deduction Period is set, the system ignores manually-entered entries in the Deductions panel for taxable wage calculations.

  • If the Deduction Period is blank, the system recognizes manually-entered deductions for taxable wage calculations.

One example for using this form is when the client does business in a state where the law requires that the employee receive a final check upon termination. You can calculate the net amount of the check after all taxes and deductions.

Important! PrismHR strongly recommends that you communicate any pay check calculations and final employee check distributions with your payroll departments as soon as possible to ensure that all payroll process issues are taken into account.

Note: Note: To restrict user access to only the calculation function, open the appropriate User Role and set the form access to Inquiry. Users with that user role will be able to perform calculations but cannot create vouchers or checks.

Use the Net Pay option in the Calculation Type to calculate the net pay for the employee. This does not initialize a voucher; use it to simply calculate the employee’s net pay amount; see Calculating Net Pay.

Making a Pay Rate Change for an Employee

Source: https://tech-docs.prismhr.com/SP_help/Content/PD/HR/Action/Pay_Rate_Change.htm Path: PD/HR/Action/Pay_Rate_Change.htm

Making a Pay Rate Change for an Employee

You can set a new pay rate for an employee using the Pay Rate Change form.

Note: Note: After you calculate a regularly scheduled payroll and then make a pay rate change for an effective date within the pay period dates of that payroll, a message displays warning you that the current payroll is in a status that does not allow for automatic pay rate updates to the timesheet. In this situation, you need to manually apply the new pay rate to any hours on the payroll using the Detail Time Sheet Entry form. (See Making Detail Time Sheet Entries.)

To make a pay rate change, do the following:

  1. From HR|Action, select Pay Rate Change.

  2. Enter the Employee by either entering the name, Social Security Number, or ID to display a pop-up list of matching employee records. You can also click the field label or press Ctrl+Enter with your cursor in this field to open the search window. The current employment information displays, and any history of pay rate changes displays at the bottom of the form.

Note: Note: The New Pay Rate and Per basis define the new employee’s pay. For example, $250 Weekly or $3500 Monthly.

  1. Enter the New Pay Rate, then select the period.

  2. Enter the New Standard Hours, then select the period. The system calculates and displays the New Annualized Pay.

  3. Select Auto Pay Time Card to populate the employee’s time sheet with hours.

  4. If the employee’s time sheet populates with time, enter the default number of hours per pay period in the New Auto Time Card Hours field.

  5. Enter the Effective Date, which is when the pay rate change goes into effect.

  • In cases where the date of the pay rate change falls during a payroll where hours have already been entered for the employee, the system allows you to enter the number of hours to pay the employee at the new rate. If you enter any hours, including 0, the option If you do not know what hours to assign to the new pay rate… is deselected and the payroll processor does not see a notification of the pay rate change in the time sheet.

  • If you do not know the number of hours to assign, leave the Hours Paid field blank. The payroll processor will be prompted to assign the hours for the employee the next time they edit the payroll.

[Image: screenshot]

  1. Select the Reason that best describes the pay rate change.

  2. Click Save.

Working with the Actions Menu

You can access related features by selecting them from the Actions menu.

  • Reason Codes: see Creating Reason Codes

  • Status Type History: see Viewing Employment Status Updates for Employees

Working with Planned Time Off Requests

Source: https://tech-docs.prismhr.com/SP_help/Content/PD/HR/Action/Planned_Time_Off_Requests.htm Path: PD/HR/Action/Planned_Time_Off_Requests.htm

Working with Planned Time Off Requests

You can review and make changes to employees’ time off (PTO) requests in the Planned Time Off Requests form.

Important! The system circumvents leave approval security configurations and workflows for users with access to this form, which can result in unexpected behavior.

Please note the following security used by this form:

  • Users with the PTO Administrator H/R Role and access to Manage Planned Time Off Requests can update leave for all employees in the client regardless of their Data Security access or the assigned PTO Approver.

  • Users without the PTO Administrator H/R Role and with access to Manage Planned Time Off Requests can update leave only for employees where the user has access via Data Security, regardless of the assigned PTO Approver.

Viewing Planned Time Off Requests

The requests in the list are for employees at the active client who submitted them through Employee Portal (EP).

To view the planned time off requests for the client, do the following:

  1. Go to the Planned Time Off Requests form. When the form opens, the following message displays, “Please wait while we get the leave requests.” (Note: This form defaults to the Display Pending and Display Approved options, so the system will display those entries.)

  2. Use the filters at the top of the form to specify which requests to display.

  3. If there is more than one page of results, the total number of pages displays above the results, and the Next Page and Previous Page buttons display at the bottom of the page. Long comments or multiple vouchers display additional lines on the form.

Note: Note: Each page displays up to 20 leave requests.

Making Changes to an Employee’s Time Off

To make changes to an employee’s time off, you can:

  • Set an Approved request to Cancelled.

  • Set an Approved request to Paid. If you select Paid, then it is removed from this list and from any payrolls.

  • A yellow indicator [Image: screenshot] displays next to the start date if it is before the period end date of the last completed scheduled payroll batch for the employee’s pay group. The system considers it to be a “back dated” request.

The different statuses affect the system as follows:

Status Description
Pending The Time Off request has not yet been approved, cancelled, or paid by the employee’s manager. Note: The request does not display on the timesheet in this status.
Approved The Time Off request is approved and scheduled to be paid. > Note: Note the following: - If a manager cancels a Time Off request on this form and then the request is approved later on this form, the leave request indicator in the Summary Time Sheet Entry form is restored and the employee’s EP Time Off landing page displays the approved request. - The system does not record the user who approves a Time Off request as the leave approver in the Planned Time Off Requests form. Instead, the system displays the approver from the approval policy.
  • If a manager cancels a Time Off request on this form and then the request is approved later on this form, the leave request indicator in the Summary Time Sheet Entry form is restored and the employee’s EP Time Off landing page displays the approved request.
  • The system does not record the user who approves a Time Off request as the leave approver in the Planned Time Off Requests form. Instead, the system displays the approver from the approval policy. | Cancelled | The Time Off request is cancelled. This cancels an employee’s approved Time Off in the EP that occurs in the future. > Note: Note the following: - Canceling approved Time Off that occurs in the future stops the Time Off from being applied to the timesheet when it is created. - Canceling approved and applied Time Off on the current timesheet does not remove the indicator and the hours should be manually adjusted in the timesheet. |
  • Canceling approved Time Off that occurs in the future stops the Time Off from being applied to the timesheet when it is created.
  • Canceling approved and applied Time Off on the current timesheet does not remove the indicator and the hours should be manually adjusted in the timesheet. | Paid | The Time Off request was previously paid and the employee’s Time Off register was updated. Only service providers can select this option. Note: This status removes the leave request indicator from the timesheet and removes the request from the employee’s EP or Employee Portal (EP) Time Off landing page. It also hides the request from the Planned Time Off Requests form unless you choose to Display Paid. | > Note: Note: By default, worksite manager and trusted advisor users can only access employees who report to them, as indicated in the Employee Details Work tab. Users can be set to see all employees at the client that they are allowed to access by giving them the PTO Administrator account management role in the Users form. Note that if you want a user to be able to view requests but not approve them, the user role must have Inquiry permission for Planned Time Off Requests.

To view a planned time off request’s changes, click the Audit Log icon

[Image: screenshot]

. The Audit Log Display opens, where you can click a log to display its changes. (See Viewing the Audit Log.)

Actions Menu

You can process approved, backdated leave requests for salaried employees by selecting Backdated Leave Request Processing from the Actions menu

[Image: screenshot]

.

See Working with Backdated Leave Requests
